Architecture Decision Records
Formal ADRs for AXIS (product + the repo root). Status values: Accepted unless noted. Dates are conceptual product epochs, not git archaeology.

ADR-001: Pluggable unified registry
Context
Early AXIS registered sources/streams/engines in separate ad hoc maps. Operators and authors needed one mental model and one install path.
Decision
Adopt a unified PluginRegistry (src/plugins/registry.ts) with kinds:
source | stream | engine | storage | component(reserved)
Every plugin shares PluginBase (id, name, kind, description, version, builtIn, configSchema, capabilities, lifecycle hooks). Registration is ordered; listeners observe register/unregister.
Consequences
Active selection lives in the Solid store, not the registry.
Built-ins protected from casual unregister.
Catalogs (sources/catalog.ts, …) become registration facades.
Contract docs under Plugins.
---
ADR-002: AXIS ≠ engine
Context
Closed chart hosts couple rendering and language runtime. That blocks offline mode, alternate evaluators, and headless reuse of PYNE.
Decision
AXIS never embeds a closed interpreter. All evaluation goes through EnginePlugin.run. UI modules call getActiveEngine() / runAndApply, not Python bindings directly (except inside the pyodide engine module).
Consequences
Language fidelity owned by PYNE.
AXIS docs do not duplicate grammar theory.
New engines (tiny demo, remote Worker, future WASM) are additive plugins.
---
ADR-003: Solid + Vite product UI
Context
Pre-2.21.0 vanilla JS shell (main.js, style.css) mixed DOM wiring with business logic and aged TV-blue tokens. Removed in 2.21.0.
Decision
Product path is SolidJS + Vite + Tailwind 4, entry src/index.tsx / app.tsx. Imperative chart library (lightweight-charts) is isolated in ChartHost / PaneManager so Solid reconciliation does not thrash canvas DOM.
Consequences
Legacy files retained for smoke/static tests only (LEGACY.md).
Icons via lucide-solid.
Deploy artifact is Vite dist/, not repo-root static tree.
---
ADR-004: Built-in engines
Context
Researchers need server fidelity, an edge evaluator, and offline demos. A single transport cannot satisfy all three.
Decision
Ship three built-in engines (BUILTIN_ENGINES in src/engines/catalog.ts). pyne-worker is builtIn: true. It is not a URL you paste onto the server engine.
| Id | Role |
| --- | --- |
| server | WebSocket /ws/run, then POST {endpoint}/run. Default endpoint https://pynescript.online (override to local Flask :5002 or the AXIS Worker). |
| pyne-worker | HOOX edge evaluator. Same /run contract, default origin the production pyne-worker. |
| pyodide | In-browser Python + vendored pynescript / antlr wheels. |
All three implement EnginePlugin and return RunResult.
Consequences
Endpoint field only for server-like engines.
Pyodide requires correct static asset deployment (ZIP integrity checks).
Timeouts unified at runner layer.
---
ADR-005: Declarative configSchema
Context
Per-plugin settings UIs do not scale; custom React/Solid forms per plugin forks the manager.
Decision
Plugins declare optional configSchema: map of field name → { type, default, label, options, min, max, ... }. Settings and library panels resolve defaults + pluginsConfig overrides via shared resolve helpers.
Consequences
New plugins get settings UI without shell changes (within field types).
Types: string | number | boolean | select initially.
Invalid user values remain a plugin responsibility at runtime.
---
ADR-006: URL-loadable plugins
Context
Third-party sources/engines should not require rebuild of the PWA for experiments.
Decision
Support loadPluginFromUrl: fetch ES module, validate shape, register, persist install metadata for restore on boot. Example plugins published under public/plugins/.
Consequences
Browser origin trust model: URL plugins are code execution.
Security tests constrain schemes and storage-via-URL footguns.
Offline restore needs prior successful fetch/cache.
---
ADR-007: Storage as a plugin
Context
Script library began as localStorage-only; cloud and git were bolted differently.
Decision
Storage is a first-class plugin kind with list/read/write/remove (+ optional draft/sync/status). Built-ins: local (IDB), cloud (Worker /api/scripts), git (GitHub/GitLab Contents API). Active storage in activePlugins.storage.
Consequences
Manager Script Library is backend-agnostic.
Git commits only on explicit save; drafts local.
Cloud uses Bearer Pro keys and optional optimistic concurrency.
---
ADR-008: Frozen CF project id
Context
Renaming Cloudflare® Pages/Worker projects severs KV/D1/R2 bindings, custom domains, and CI secrets. Product brand evolved to AXIS. The original freeze used a single id pynescript-axis for both Pages and the Worker.
Decision
Pages project is axis. Canonical URL https://axis.hoox.sh (custom domain). Preview hashes live on .axis.pages.dev. The previous Pages id pynescript-axis remains a CORS legacy alias for old preview hosts.
Worker script is worker-axis. Canonical URL https://worker.axis.hoox.sh (custom domain). Health JSON service is worker-axis. The pre-rename workers.dev host (pynescript-axis..workers.dev) is treated as a legacy alias in client host matching.
Brand in UI/manifest/docs is AXIS.
Consequences
wrangler.toml name is worker-axis; Pages --project-name is axis.
Canonical hosts: Pages https://axis.hoox.sh, Worker https://worker.axis.hoox.sh.
Docs call out the split explicitly (this ADR).
npm package name (axis-worker) may lag brand.
---
ADR-009: Worker proxies first
Context
In-Worker Pyodide is attractive but heavy; production needed a path immediately.
Decision
Worker /api/run proxies to an external Python backend first (EXTERNAL_BACKEND). In-Worker runtime remains scaffolded/gated. Keys, usage metering, scripts API, and stream DO can ship independently of full edge evaluation.
Consequences
AXIS server engine talks to Worker URL transparently.
Operational dependency on Flask (or equivalent) until edge runtime matures.
Clear layering: Worker is data plane + auth, not necessarily the interpreter.
---
ADR-010: Durable Object stream fan-out
Context
N browser clients each opening venue WebSockets waste connections and risk rate limits.
Decision
SessionDO Durable Object: one upstream WS per session key; fan-out to N client sockets via /api/stream. Hibernation-friendly design on CF.
Consequences
Stream plugins may target DO URLs (example plugin provided).
Session/symbol/interval query params are part of the contract.
Not a historical source—live only.
---
ADR-011: State namespace migration
Context
Hard-renaming storage namespaces would brick user layouts and libraries if keys changed hard.
Decision
Canonical key pynescript.axis.v1. On read, fall back to pynescript.axis.v2 (and parallel library/draft keys), then write forward. Do not keep dual-write forever.
Consequences
Seamless upgrade for existing browsers.
Persistence strips bars/lastRun/logs.
Tests cover migration (tests/state.test.ts patterns).
---
ADR-012: CORS as deploy concern
Context
Browser AXIS on origin A calling Pro API on origin B fails without CORS. Hardcoding origins in AXIS is wrong.
Decision
CORS is a backend/deploy configuration (ALLOWED_ORIGINS on Flask; Worker headers as deployed). AXIS documents required origins; ops sets explicit lists in production (avoid perpetual ` outside demos).
Consequences
Localhost regex for dev.
Troubleshooting centers on preflight, not engine code.
Static file server does not replace API CORS.
---
ADR-013: Results export in AXIS
Context
Researchers need artifacts (trades CSV, run JSON) without a separate analytics service.
Decision
Results panel owns export: download JSON of lastRun, CSV of closed trades, clipboard helpers. Strategy pairing and stats run client-side from engine events (results/strategy.ts, results/events.ts).
Consequences
Export fidelity bounded by engine event quality.
No server-side report store required for MVP.
Viewer metrics ≠ broker statements (documented for operators).
---
ADR-014: Transport preference + connection telemetry
Context
Operators need to see how data and calculation move (WS vs REST vs local vs brokered), whether the live socket is actually open, and engine run latency. Naively “prefer WebSocket everywhere” is wrong: venues expose history over REST and live klines over WSS. Server engines batch-run over HTTP; Pyodide is local WASM.
Decision
1. Transport policy
History → REST (or local mock/CSV).
Live → WebSocket first, paired per source via defaultStreamForSource (binance-rest → binance-ws, …).
Engine → prefer WS /ws/run on the Pro API when available (flask-sock); fall back to HTTP POST /run. Pyodide remains local.
Brokered → CF Durable Object / needsProxy streams surface as transport class broker.
2. Honest stream state — connecting until onStatus({ state: 'open' }); reconnect uses reconnecting / degraded telemetry, not false green.
3. Venue streams use reconnect with exponential backoff (streams/reconnect-ws.ts).
4. Connection HUD — StatusBar second row (ConnectionHud) reads ephemeral store.telemetry (SRC/STR/ENG/STO chips, tick pulse, engine latency). Persist only telemetry.hud prefs + live.preferAfterLoad / live.rerunOn.
5. Live re-run modes — every-tick (default) or bar-close (venue Bar.closed or bar time advance).
6. Chart stability — full setDataToChart only on history load (chartDataGen); live uses appendBar; overlay/script drawings update in place to avoid hide/show flash.
Consequences
UI never claims WS for historical load.
Auto-live after Load is on by default (preferAfterLoad). Turn off in Settings → Live stream.
Plugin capabilities may declare transport; otherwise HUD classifies from id/capabilities.
See Streams, UI shell, Overview.
---
ADR-015: On-chain data plane
Context
AXIS began as an OHLCV-first chart host: SourcePlugin / StreamPlugin deliver Bar[] (open, high, low, close, volume) for CEX and similar venues, then engines run Pine against that series. Operators also need non-price market structure:
| Need | Why Bar alone fails |
| --- | --- |
| Protocol TVL (USD) | Single scalar liquidity total, not OHLC |
| DEX pool candles | Same shape as bars, but different venue semantics, address/network keys, and page caps |
| Spike / unlock-style events | Discrete time markers with severity, not candles |
| Honest provenance | Provider, snapshot cadence, finality — not implied by “the chart symbol” |
Forcing these into the primary Bar pipeline would corrupt scale semantics (TVL vs price), invent fake OHLC for scalar metrics, and couple wallet/signing UX to read-only analytics.
Decision
Ship a parallel on-chain data plane orthogonal to the OHLCV source/stream path:
1. Plugin kind dataset on the unified registry (DatasetPlugin in src/plugins/types.ts):
fetchDataset(opts) → OnchainDataset
optional searchInstruments
payload kinds: ohlcv | scalar_series | multi_series | events | table
required provenance + finality (pending | safe | finalized | unknown)
2. Parallel plane modules under src/onchain/ (types, cache, keys, adapters, catalog, manager, events, alerts bridge) — not a new Bar field and not Pine output.
3. Worker allowlist proxy (worker/src/onchain.ts, routes under /api/onchain/…):
rewrite only known DefiLlama / GeckoTerminal paths
validate network ids and pool addresses
short-TTL isolate cache (X-Axis-Onchain-Cache)
client default bases from store.endpoint (src/onchain/proxy.ts)
4. Chart UX: attach as host-side overlays (left scale for scalars; separate series keys onchain_); main market OHLCV stays on the right scale. Not wallet, not Pine plots.
Alternatives considered
| Alternative | Rejected because |
| --- | --- |
| Force into Bar / active source | TVL is not OHLC; faking open=high=low=close lies to engines and the price scale. Multi-protocol attach becomes multi-symbol load thrash. |
| Piggyback Pine request.security only | Needs engine + script for every host overlay; no attach UI, no provider search, no finality UI. |
| Browser → provider direct | Public DefiLlama / GeckoTerminal hosts often lack CORS for arbitrary AXIS origins (ADR-012). |
| Open reverse proxy (any URL) | SSRF risk; Worker must allowlist path shape, not pass-through arbitrary upstream. |
| Wallet-connected RPC as the plane | Different product (signing, accounts, private RPC). On-chain here means public analytics APIs, not MetaMask. |
Consequences
CORS / deploy — browsers should use {endpoint}/api/onchain/llama|gecko; local Worker on :8787 or deployed edge. Direct baseUrl overrides are for Node tests / trusted environments only.
Finality honesty — DefiLlama TVL is typically a daily snapshot, not block-final chain state; Gecko OHLCV is pool sampling, not CEX marks. UI shows provenance lines; synthetic flags fabricated OHLC.
Not a wallet — no signing, no injected providers, no private keys for this plane.
Not Pine — overlays are host datasets; they do not replace indicator() / strategy() plots from the engine.
Engines unchanged — active OHLCV source still feeds EnginePlugin.run; on-chain series do not silently become script close.
Cache — client IDB dataset cache (instrumentCacheKey) plus Worker short-TTL memory; operators may see stale TVL briefly after large DeFi moves.
Security — Worker rejects path traversal, invalid slugs/addresses, non-GET, and non-allowlisted Gecko paths.
Phases shipped
| Phase | Capability | Primary surfaces |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 — TVL | DefiLlama protocol TVL scalar history + search | defillama-tvl dataset, src/onchain/defillama.ts, llama proxy |
| 2 — DEX | GeckoTerminal pool OHLCV + pool search | source geckoterminal-ohlcv, src/onchain/geckoterminal.ts, gecko proxy. Not a DatasetPlugin. |
| 3 — Events | Derived day-over-day TVL spike/drop events (tvl_spike / tvl_drop) | src/onchain/events.ts → chart markers |
| 4 — Alerts | Bridge events into alerts engine (onchain_tvl_spike / onchain_event) | src/onchain/alerts-bridge.ts, src/alerts/engine.ts |
Pro-only DefiLlama surfaces (raises, unlocks via pro-api.llama.fi) remain out of the free Worker allowlist.
Code map
| Path | Role |
| --- | --- |
| src/onchain/types.ts | OnchainDataset, instruments, finality |
| src/onchain/catalog.ts | Built-in dataset registration |
| src/onchain/manager.ts | Attach / hide / detach store wiring |
| src/onchain/proxy.ts | Resolve Worker llama/gecko bases |
| worker/src/onchain.ts | Allowlisted proxy + health |
| src/plugins/types.ts | DatasetPlugin contract |
Related docs
Datasets — plugin contract
On-Chain data (end user) — operator guide
Worker — /api/onchain/… on the edge route table
ADR-001, ADR-009, ADR-012
---
ADR-016: Provider-locked market data
Context
AXIS composes source × stream × engine, but OHLCV aggregation is not composable. Mixing Binance history with OKX live (or Kraken watchlist quotes on a Binance chart) silently poisons indicators, compare, watchlist, and cache. Prior to this ADR, source and stream were independently selectable, venue detection used plugin-id substrings, and the Binance synthetic fallback made network errors look like real data.
Key problems:
| Issue | Risk |
| --- | --- |
| Source and stream independently selectable in Topbar | User loads Binance REST + OKX WS — mixed candles |
| DSM sourceId seeded once from store.source | Backfill targets a different venue than the chart |
| Watchlist Kraken → Binance tickers | Mixed-provider quotes |
| Coinbase WS ticker → synthetic bars | Not exchange candles; OHLCV ≠ REST history |
| Binance fallback: true synthesizes random walk | Chart looks "live" with fake prices |
Decision
Introduce ProviderSession — a first-class locked market-data identity that all aggregators inherit:
1. ProviderSession (src/data/provider.ts): { id, sourceId, streamId, venue, market, authMode, credentialId, gateway }. Persisted without secrets. Every aggregator reads getActiveProvider() instead of raw URLs or plugin-id substrings.
2. Auto-pairing: Source changes always re-pair the stream via defaultStreamForSource. Explicit mismatch is allowed but shows a HUD pair · Fix chip.
3. Plugin capabilities: venue, market, klineStream on PluginCapabilities. Venues with klineStream: true emit exchange candles (not ticker buckets).
4. Coinbase WS: switched from ticker buckets to Advanced Trade venue candles, folded into chart interval via foldVenueCandle.
5. Binance fallback: defaults off (fallback: false). Network errors must not look like real data.
6. Watchlist lock: no silent Binance fallback for Kraken, Gecko, or unknown venues. Each shows a clear "no live mux" message.
7. Credential vault (src/data/credentials.ts): in-memory session-only vault for API key/secret/passphrase. Secrets never reach persist(), pluginsConfig, error-share, or CHANGELOG dumps. redactSecrets and stripSecretKeys enforce this.
8. Venue HMAC signers (src/data/venues/): thin Binance / OKX / Bybit / Coinbase / Kraken / MEXC signers. No CCXT in the browser.
9. Signed Binance klines: vault key → HMAC direct fetch → Worker /api/market/binance/signed/klines (request-scoped X-Exchange-Key / X-Exchange-Secret). 401/403 stays thrown — bad keys must not silently mix public klines.
10. Cache key: includes providerId|market|authMode|symbol|interval. Authenticated and public candles do not share cache slots.
Ownership
`
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ AXIS (composition host) │
│ ProviderSession (venue + market + auth mode + credential ref) │
│ SourcePlugin ── REST history / pagination / DSM / compare │
│ StreamPlugin ── venue kline WS (not ticker-synthesized bars) │
│ Credential vault (browser session / optional persist / Tauri) │
│ Datafeed Gateway client → pyne | worker | sidecar │
└───────────────┬─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
│ │
▼ ▼
┌───────────────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────────────────────┐
│ PYNE [data]/[datafeed] │ │ AXIS Worker │
│ CCXT + CCXT Pro │ │ Thin HMAC REST proxy (no CCXT) │
│ CLI, request., gateway │ │ DO WS fan-out (public + auth │
│ Secrets: env / key store │ │ listenKey) — allowlisted paths │
└───────────────────────────┘ └─────────────────────────────────┘
`
Alternatives considered
| Alternative | Rejected because |
| --- | --- |
| Bundle CCXT in browser | ~1 MB+ bundle, Node APIs, isolate CPU, CORS issues |
| CCXT in Cloudflare® Worker | Bundle size, 128 MB memory, shared rate-limit IP, not designed for 100-exchange routing |
| Keep venues as raw URL substrings | Silent venue mismatches poison aggregation correctness |
| Pluggable venue resolver | Over-engineered; first-party venues are a fixed set of 5 |
Consequences
Correctness — aggregation (indicators, compare, watchlist, cache, DSM) inherits a locked venue. Mixed-provider bugs are structurally prevented.
Auth path — public remains the default. Auth is a capability upgrade on the same provider, not a second source id.
Secret safety — vault is RAM-only in v1. Tauri keychain / AES-GCM durable wrap is Phase 1.5. pluginsConfig and error-share are stripped of secret keys.
CCXT stays in PYNE — server-side only. AXIS calls the gateway with session handles, never raw secrets in /run.
Long-tail exchanges — AXIS ccxt-rest / ccxt-ws plugins that talk to a PYNE or Bun sidecar gateway. First-party venues stay thin native adapters.
Key files
| Path | Role |
| --- | --- |
| src/data/provider.ts | ProviderSession type, buildProviderSession, defaultStreamForSource |
| src/data/credentials.ts | In-memory vault, putCredential, redactSecrets |
| src/data/signed-fetch.ts | Vault → HMAC → direct or Worker proxy |
| src/data/venues/ | Per-venue HMAC signers (binance, okx, bybit, coinbase, kraken) |
| src/plugins/active.ts | getActiveProvider() |
| src/plugins/types.ts | PluginCapabilities.venue, .market, .klineStream |
| src/store/index.ts | setActivePlugin auto-pairing, syncProviderSession |
| src/ui/ConnectionHud.tsx | Venue label, pairing Fix button |
| src/ui/SettingsDialog.tsx | Data tab (exchange credentials) |
| src/ui/error-share.ts | stripSecretKeys redaction |
| worker/src/market.ts | Signed Binance klines proxy |

ADR-001, ADR-009

ADR-017: Narrow bar plane
Context
AppState.bars is a single Solid store array. A live tick that replaces or mutates store.bars notifies every createEffect / createMemo that read store.bars or store.bars.length — document title, status bar, quote card, volume profile, ChartHost overlays, even when they only need the forming tip. Phase 1 of the Q4 2026 perf audit measured that as the dominant reactivity cost on the live path.
Keeping one giant array as both the engine input and the UI subscription surface cannot stay under an 8 ms tick→paint budget at 5k–50k bars.
Decision
Introduce a narrow bar plane at src/stores/bars.ts (barPlane) with four independent fields:
| Field | Updates when | Typical readers |
| --- | --- | --- |
| bars | full load, slot restore, rolling-window slide | engine apply, full-window paint |
| tip | every accepted live tick (cloned, never the same object as bars[n-1]) | last price, title, quote, HUD |
| length | grow or slide, not forming-tick overwrite | chrome that only needs count |
| gen | full reload / slide | result cache key, overlay invalidation |
Write path:
loadBars / slot restore → setBarPlaneFull
in-place append → setBarPlaneAppend (no whole-window copy)
forming tick → barPlane.tip only (flushLiveFrame)
store.bars remains the durable engine/eval source during migration. New UI readers subscribe to barPlane, not store.bars.
The tip is cloned on full-window writes so the Solid store proxy for barPlane.tip cannot alias an element of store.bars (that aliasing broke closed mutation in store-closed-on-new-bar.test.ts).
Alternatives considered
| Alternative | Rejected because |
| --- | --- |
| Fine-grained signals on store.bars[i] | Solid store array replace still notifies length/identity readers; does not split tip from window. |
| Drop store.bars immediately | Engine eval, DSM, and ChartHost paint still consume the full window; a hard cut would fork persistence. |
| Shared typed-array buffer | Follow-up (Phase 2 GC hygiene). Plane first, buffers later. |
Consequences
Live ticks that only move the forming OHLC must not bump barPlane.gen or copy barPlane.bars.
Overlay/result caches key off barPlane.gen + barPlane.length, not array identity.
Persistence still serializes store.bars. Do not persist the plane separately.
ChartHost's full paint path may keep reading store.bars until a later phase moves it.
Key files
| Path | Role |
| --- | --- |
| src/stores/bars.ts | barPlane, setBarPlaneFull, setBarPlaneAppend |
| src/store/index.ts | loadBars / appendBar / flushLiveFrame writers |
| src/app.tsx, src/ui/StatusBar.tsx, src/ui/Topbar.tsx | migrated readers |
| src/indicators/run-cache.ts | cache key includes barsGen + length |
Related docs
Performance audit — Phase 1 notes
State namespaces
ADR-011
---
ADR-018: Engine result parse + typed contract
Context
Engine responses (POST /run, /ws/run, Pyodide run) return one JSON string that can be hundreds of KB to several MB (equity, plot series, trades). JSON.parse of a 10k-bar payload was measured at p99 ~7 ms on the main thread — most of the 8 ms tick→apply budget — before apply even starts. Python json.dumps can also emit bare NaN / Infinity, which standard JSON rejects.
A second cost: REST, WebSocket, and Pyodide used to present slightly different result shapes to the apply pipeline, so each path re-normalized plots, trades, and equity.
True chunked streaming of Pyodide evaluation is still outstanding (Phase 5 backlog). This ADR covers the parse/contract slice that already shipped.
Decision
1. Shared sanitizing parser (src/engines/json-sanitize.ts):
sanitizeNonFiniteJson rewrites bare NaN / Infinity / -Infinity to null without touching string literals.
parseEngineJson is the synchronous path (small bodies, 413 peeks, tests).
parseEngineJsonAsync parses payloads ≥ 256 KB in a module Worker (src/engines/json-parse-worker.ts). Below the threshold, or when Worker is unavailable, it falls back to parseEngineJson.
2. Typed apply contract: every engine path lifts through normalizeEngineResult (src/indicators/run-helpers.ts) to a NormalizedRunResult before ChartHost/apply. Pyodide, REST /run, WS /ws/run, and /run/batch share that shape.
3. In-memory result cache (src/indicators/run-cache.ts): successful non-live runs keyed by (engineId, scriptId, scriptHash, inputs, config, barsGen, barsLength, libraries). Bounded 16 entries / 30 s TTL; cloned on store and retrieve; disabled in tests and for live ticks.
4. Not this ADR: incremental Pyodide line/mark streaming (TransformStream). Parse-off-thread of the final JSON string is the current win.
Alternatives considered
| Alternative | Rejected because |
| --- | --- |
| Always parse on main thread | 10k OHLCV p99 ~7 ms already saturates the tick budget. |
| Always off-thread | Worker postMessage of a 1 KB status frame is slower than JSON.parse. Threshold 256 KB. |
| Streaming JSON parser on main | Extra dependency; Worker reuse is enough for the final blob. |
| SharedArrayBuffer / typed-array wire | Needs backend envelope changes; gated for a later contract rev. |
Consequences
Callers of parseEngineJsonAsync must await. Sync peeks stay on parseEngineJson.
Worker startup is amortized via a shared module worker; tests call _resetParseEngineJsonForTests.
Cache must not serve live ticks or mutated apply payloads (clone on write/read).
Chunked Pyodide evaluation remains future work; do not advertise partial plots as complete.
Key files
| Path | Role |
| --- | --- |
| src/engines/json-sanitize.ts | parseEngineJson, parseEngineJsonAsync, 256 KB threshold |
| src/engines/json-parse-worker.ts | Off-thread JSON.parse after sanitize |
| src/indicators/run-helpers.ts | normalizeEngineResult / NormalizedRunResult |
| src/indicators/run-cache.ts | Bounded memo of non-live runs |
| src/engines/catalog.ts, live-sessions.ts, engine-ws.ts, batch.ts` | Call sites |
Related docs
Performance audit — Phase 3 notes

ADR lifecycle
New ADRs should:
1. State context, decision, consequences.
2. Cross-link code paths.
3. Avoid re-litigating ADR-002 without supersession note.

Drawings and plot parity
AXIS has two independent render paths:
| Path | Source | Paint |
|---|---|---|
| User drawings | Toolbar tools | DrawingLayer user SVG group, editable |
| Pine output | PYNE RunResult | plots → LWC series; shapes/barcolor → candle markers; bgcolor → histogram; fill → SVG; line/label/box/polyline/linefill → script SVG; tables → chart HUD or bottom Tables panel |
This plan is the work list from a four-agent audit of AXIS (src/chart, src/results, src/indicators/runner.ts) against PYNE (pynescript drawing.py, evaluator.py, host.py).
Phases
P0 — AXIS correctness (this pass)
User tools and apply-pipeline bugs that do not wait on a PYNE export change.
1. 1-point body-move — shiftDrawing only special-cased text / priceLabel. Shift any drawing with p1 (and points[] when present): note, flag, crossline, anchoredText, arrowMarkUp/Down.
2. p3 / extra vertex handles — hitTestHandle / resizeDrawing only p1/p2. Add p3 (and remaining points[i] as p3 for the third anchor). Paint extra handles after registry paint.
3. Linefill GC — garbageCollectScriptDrawings early-return ignored skip.linefill.
4. Polyline / linefill overlay — copy force_overlay and polyline fill_color → bgcolor.
5. Stale SVG — silent live re-run with drawings: [] must clear script drawings.
6. Pane routing — bgcolor and fill() use the script pane when overlay=false (syncBgcolorBands(paneId), ensureScriptPaneLayer for fills). plotshape / plotchar / plotarrow attach to a series on that pane when possible; otherwise stay on price (LWC markers need a host series).
P1 — User-tool settings (AXIS)
Done:
Shared style bar: color (presets + custom), width, dash, fill for shapes / ranges / fib / highlighter / positions.
Per-tool extras via src/chart/drawings/tool-settings.ts + settings popover (axis-drawing-settings).
Last-used style per kind (drawingPrefs.byKind).
extendLeft / extendRight UI + paint (trend, ray, extend, info line, channel, fib).
Fib level editor, reverse, show % / price, fill between levels.
Text re-edit (popover + double-click), font size.
Per-drawing lock in the style bar (unlock bypasses updateSelected lock gate).
Long/short risk-reward (meta.rr).
Highlighter width honors style.width (create default 8).
hray: 1-click (price only).
xabcd / headShoulders: commit on 5th point.
P2 — PYNE export (pynescript repo)
AXIS cannot paint fields the engine never serializes.
| Gap | Where | Needed on the wire |
|---|---|---|
| Table cell keys (row,col) vs (col,row) | drawing.py table.cell vs cell_set_ | One index order |
| Table style | export_for_api | halign, valign, width, tooltip, text_size, borders (AXIS already parses width / height / tooltip / merged_cells / force_overlay when present; bottom panel uses them for proportional columns, hover tips, and merges) |
| table.cell kwargs | ctor | width, halign, valign, size |
| plotshape Y / location.absolute | _coerce_plot_shape | keep numeric series (or {y, on} cells) |
| plotbar / plotcandle | evaluator | open/high/low/close (or sibling titles in plot_meta) |
| fill() compile / untitled plots | host + evaluator | plot1/plot2 series keys + per-bar fill color column |
| Positional plot(..., linewidth) | evaluator.py ~425 | Pine order: series, title, color, linewidth, style |
| plotarrow | evaluator | colorup / colordown / minheight / maxheight |
| offset, force_overlay, histbase | _pack_interpret_plot_columns | remaining plot_meta fields (display is packed) |
| Polyline | export | fill_color, curved, force_overlay |
| Box / label extras | export | closed, extend, text_, tooltip, border |
P3 — Remaining Pine paint
Done this pass:
Hide-drawings toggle includes script SVG + fill bands (all pane layers).
Label yloc pad (8px off wick); label.style_text_outline as outlined text.
First addIndicator re-owns plot fills / script drawings / plotshape (EDITOR_RUN_KEY → script id).
Histogram histbase from plot_meta (default 0). PYNE still needs to serialize it for live values.
Pine display. (none / pane / data_window / price_scale / status_line / all) on plot, hline, shapes, fill, bgcolor, barcolor.
Still open:
location.top / bottom = pane edges, not bar (LWC markers only have above/below/in bar).
Per-bar plot(..., color=expr), plotarrow length (colorup/colordown/minheight/maxheight wait on PYNE).
Pine tables: bottom Tables panel (shipped)
table. output paints in the chart HUD (PyneTableHud) by default. Per script, Move tables to bottom (chart ⋯ menu, pane badge ⋯, command palette) relocates that script's tables to ui/TablesPanel.tsx:
One tab per script, stacked vertically in creation order regardless of position / force_overlay.
Proportional column widths from table.cell(width=…) (bottomPanelColumnFractions in chart/pine-tables.ts), tooltips + merged cells preserved, text selectable.
Placement persists per script in store.pineTablesLocation; the HUD hides moved tables. Move back via Move tables to chart.
Invariants
User drawings ≠ Pine drawings. Do not share hit-testing or magnet with script SVG (pointer-events: none).
overlay=false plots, fills, bgcolor, and shapes belong on ind_* unless force_overlay.
barcolor() stays on price candles.
Color sanitize: #rgb(a), #rrggbb(aa), rgb/rgba. Unknown → VOID indigo for user tools; Pine should keep engine hex (including #RRGGBBAA).
GC caps match Pine: 50 default, 500 lines/labels/boxes, 100 polylines. Linefill cap is AXIS-only until PYNE emits max_lines_fills_count.
Tests
shiftDrawing / resizeDrawing for 1-pt and 3-pt kinds.
Linefill GC overflow.
Polyline force_overlay + fill_color.
Runner: bgcolor/fill target pane when overlay=false (unit with a fake manager if needed).
PYNE: table cell key, fill meta, plotshape Y (in the pynescript repo).

---
title: "Evaluation map"
description: "How AXIS runs Pine: Flask Pro API, Worker proxy, in-browser Pyodide. AXIS does not import PyneTS and does not place HOOX orders."
---
Evaluation map
Abstract
AXIS is a charting PWA, not a language rewrite. Every Pine evaluation goes through an engine plugin. Built-ins are server (HTTP/WS to PYNE) and pyodide (in-browser Python wheel). Optional HOOX pyne-worker is just another Backend URL.
AXIS does not import @hoox-sh/pynets. AXIS does not call trade-worker. Drawing a strategy on the chart is research, not execution.
Conceptual model
Interface surface
| Path | Implementation | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Engine server | src/engines/catalog.ts | Prefer WS ws(s)://{endpoint}/ws/run (preferWs default true), else POST {endpoint}/run?mode= with { script, data, mode, inputs?, profiler?, libraries? }. Modes interpret \| compile \| auto. Timeout scales with bar count (45–180s). Default endpoint https://pynescript.online (local desk override http://127.0.0.1:5002). Strategy HPO uses POST {endpoint}/optimize. Live sessions (Pro API 0.6.9+, interpret only): genuine live ticks (EngineOpts.liveTick) go through src/engines/live-sessions.ts — POST /session, /tick (O(1)), /append (O(delta)) — with silent fallback to the full run on any miss. Only engaged for interpret runs: the configured mode must be interpret, or the backend must have reported interpret for the last full run of the same script (auto). Scripts whose inputs hold expanded input.source series stay on /run — a session freezes its inputs at create. |
| Engine pyodide | same file | Self-hosted Pyodide 0.29.5 (/pyodide/v0.29.5/, Python 3.13), wheels /vendor/pynescript-0.6.30-py3-none-any.whl + antlr4_python3_runtime-4.13.2, runtime /pyodide/pynescript_runtime.py. Numba is not in Wasm; compile / auto is object-mode. Cold load ~20–30s. |
| AXIS Worker /api/run | worker/src/runtime.ts | PYODIDE_IN_WORKER=enabled scaffold; else proxy EXTERNAL_BACKEND/run; else 503 NO_BACKEND. Caps: 512 KiB script, 50k bars, 30 req/min/IP, 60s upstream. |
| Engine pyne-worker | src/engines/catalog.ts | Built-in (builtIn: true). POST /run to the HOOX edge worker. Default origin https://pyne-worker.cryptolinx.workers.dev. Distinct from engine server and from the AXIS Worker (worker-axis). |
| PyneTS | — | TypeScript library. PYNE docs. Not referenced in AXIS source. |
Payloads follow the PYNE evaluate contract. Defaults: Flask POST /run uses mode auto. See modes.
Internals
| Path | Role |
| --- | --- |
| src/engines/catalog.ts | server, pyne-worker, pyodide |
| src/engines/live-sessions.ts | O(delta) live sessions (/session, /tick, /append); interpret-only, silent fallback to /run |
| src/workers/catalog.ts | Workers page catalog entries |
| worker/src/runtime.ts | Edge /api/run |
| public/pyodide/ | Wasm runtime + wheel |
| scripts/sync-pyne-wheel.sh | Refresh vendored wheel |
Invariants & edge cases
1. AXIS ≠ engine (ADR-002). The shell never embeds a closed interpreter.
2. AXIS ≠ execution. Strategy events on the Results panel are not HOOX orders. See AXIS and HOOX.
3. No none stream plugin in src/streams/catalog.ts. Pausing live data is stopLive(), not a plugin id.
4. The vendored wheel is pynescript-0.6.30-py3-none-any.whl, built from the sister pyne repo with scripts/sync-pyne-wheel.sh. That script also prints a warning if the hard-coded path in src/engines/catalog.ts drifts from the wheel filename — update it when the version changes.
5. CORS: Worker allows localhost, .hoox.sh, .pynescript.online, .axis.pages.dev (plus legacy .pynescript-axis.pages.dev) only.
6. A live tick never changes evaluation semantics. Live sessions (src/engines/live-sessions.ts) always evaluate in interpret, so the O(delta) path engages only for a run the backend itself resolved to interpret (the client records the mode each full run reports), and a session freezes its inputs at create — scripts whose inputs hold expanded input.source series stay on POST /run. Any miss falls back to the full run, and a response that is not a /run-shaped result is a miss, never an empty success.
Worked examples
Local desk
``bash
terminal 1 — PYNE Pro API
cd ../pyne && make run # :5002 — sister repo hoox-sh/pyne; some checkouts are still named pynescript
terminal 2 — AXIS
cd axis && bun run dev
`
Manager → engine server, Backend URL http://127.0.0.1:5002 (overrides the product default https://pynescript.online).
Offline
Switch engine to pyodide. First run downloads/caches the self-hosted runtime. Do not expect Numba.
Edge proxy
Deploy AXIS Worker with EXTERNAL_BACKEND pointing at Flask or pyne-worker. Engine stays server; endpoint is the Worker origin. Still no trade-worker hop unless that backend forwards events.
Pyodide in Worker (scaffold — not production yet)
Goal: a fully-CF deployment (no Flask) with the Worker evaluating Pine itself.
`toml
worker/wrangler.toml
PYODIDE_IN_WORKER = "enabled" # default "disabled" — leave it there for now
`
Status: Pyodide boots per isolate, but the wheel install + run_script
bridge are stubbed, so every run returns an error envelope (HTTP 200) and
does not fall through to EXTERNAL_BACKEND. Enabling the flag today
breaks evaluation. Completion checklist (R2 wheel, BUNDLES binding,
micropip install, bridge, fail-open semantics) lives in
worker/RUNTIME.md. Production recommendation stays EXTERNAL_BACKEND.
Failure modes
| Symptom | Cause | Fix |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 503 NO_BACKEND | Worker has no EXTERNAL_BACKEND and Pyodide-in-worker off | Set backend or use browser Pyodide |
| 503 body { status: 'error', code: 'NO_BACKEND' } to API consumers | Same as above, machine shape | Do not retry — the backend is absent, not busy. Fix config, then resend. (PYNE Agent treats this as skipped-with-note after 1 attempt.) |
| Compile "slow / different" in Pyodide | No Numba | Use Flask for numeric compile |
| Expecting pynets in the PWA | Not wired | Import @hoox-sh/pynets` in your own app |
| Strategy PnL on chart, no CEX fill | AXIS does not execute | pyne-worker live trading |
See also
Engines
Worker runtime
PYNE Pro API
PyneTS
HOOX pyne-worker
AXIS and HOOX

---
title: "Live chart engine"
description: "Lightweight Charts paints the chart. One ingest worker owns /ws/live and decodes AXB1. The shared-memory ring and GPU overlay were removed."
---
Live chart engine
Lightweight Charts paints candles, indicators, and fills. There is no second candle painter.
One browser worker owns the engine live socket. It decodes JSON and AXB1 off the window thread and posts frames to the chart. That path is the default: axis.engine.transport unset or worker. Set the flag to json and reload to keep the main-thread client. If the worker cannot start, the chart uses that client once and counts the fallback.
What stayed
| Piece | Where | Role |
| --- | --- | --- |
| AXB1 codec | src/hp/axb1/ | Binary frames on the worker socket. Prices stay int64 ticks. |
| Ingest worker | src/hp/workers/ | Owns /ws/live, coalesces forming tips, posts decoded frames. |
| Execution context | src/hp/calc/execution-context.ts | A forming tip does not commit a closed bar. |
| Isolation headers | src/hp/isolation-headers.ts | COOP / COEP / CORP for the static hosts and the offline shell. |
What was removed
The shared-memory hot ring, the cold page slot, the WebGL2 and WebGPU candle overlay, the calc worker, the HUD chip, and the axis.engine.hp.* flags are gone. Settings has no high-performance section. hp.get, hp.sample, and dev.trace are not MCP capabilities.
Stored axis.engine.hp.enabled, axis.engine.hp.gpu, and axis.engine.hp.sab values are ignored.

title: "Architecture"
description: "AXIS system architecture: AXIS vs engine, plugin registry, topologies, ADRs, and state namespaces."
---
Architecture
AXIS architecture is the discipline of separable axes—price history, live time, calculation, and library storage—coordinated by a Solid AXIS UI that never owns a closed interpreter.
Abstract
| Layer | Responsibility |
| --- | --- |
| AXIS | UI, store, plugin orchestration, chart apply |
| Plugin contracts | source \| stream \| engine \| storage \| dataset (+ reserved component) |
| Engines | PYNE evaluation (browser Pyodide or remote /run) |
| On-chain plane | Parallel non-OHLCV datasets (TVL, DEX, events) via Worker allowlist proxy — ADR-015 |
| Bar plane | Fine-grained OHLCV signals (barPlane) separate from store.bars — ADR-017 |
| Optional edge | Cloudflare® Pages + Worker + DO/KV/D1/R2 |
| Optional desk API | Flask Pro API |
Invariant: AXIS ≠ engine. Evaluation always crosses an engine plugin boundary.
Track map
| Page | Contents |
| --- | --- |
| Overview | End-to-end data/control flow |
| ADRs | ADR-001 … ADR-018 |
| Perf audit | Q4 2026 performance & architecture plan |
| Topologies | Dev, static, edge deploy shapes |
| State namespaces | Keys, migration, hash, IDB |
| Drawings and plot parity | User tools vs Pine plots/drawings vs PYNE export |
| Live chart engine | Lightweight Charts paint, ingest worker, AXB1. The shared-memory ring and GPU overlay are gone |
Conceptual model
Formal namespaces
``text
Contract namespace: pynescript.axis.plugins.v1
App state key: pynescript.axis.v1
App state prefix: pynescript.axis.*
Pages project id: axis
Pages URL: https://axis.hoox.sh
Worker script id: worker-axis
Worker URL: https://worker.axis.hoox.sh
Health service id: worker-axis
`
Related tracks
End User — operator workflows
UI — UI subsystem design
Plugins — contracts & catalogs
Worker — edge data plane
DevOps — build & CORS
PYNE runtime — language evaluation
See also
Root README.md — package-level map
LEGACY.md` — pre-Solid shell boundary
AXIS CLI — operator install / deploy

title: "Architecture overview"
description: "End-to-end AXIS architecture: Solid AXIS UI, unified registry, three built-in engines, chart apply pipeline, and optional edge plane."
---
Architecture overview
Abstract
AXIS is a composition host. The product thesis: own the axes (history, live, calc, library), swap the implementations. Shipping UI is Solid + Vite + lightweight-charts + CodeMirror 6; calculation is never inlined into UI components.
Conceptual model
Interface surface (module map)
| Concern | Primary paths |
| --- | --- |
| Entry | src/index.tsx, app.tsx |
| State | src/store/ |
| Plugins | src/plugins/{registry,types,loader,bootstrap,active}.ts |
| Catalogs | sources/catalog.ts, streams/, engines/catalog.ts, storage/ |
| Chart | chart/ChartHost.tsx, pane-manager.ts, drawing-layer.ts, price-precision.ts |
| Editor | editor/ |
| Run apply | indicators/runner.ts |
| Results | results/, ui/ResultsPanel.tsx |
| Studio pages | ui/studio/, ui/runtime/, ui/wire/, ui/settings/, ui/workers/, ui/plugins/ |
| Workers catalog | workers/ |
| Worker | worker/ |
| CLI | packages/cli/ |
| Desktop | src-tauri/, src/desktop/ |
Pre-Solid parallel (state.js, registry.js, main.js) was removed in 2.21.0 — Solid + Vite is the only product path. See repo LEGACY.md.
Control plane vs data plane
| Plane | What moves | Who owns it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Control | activePlugins, endpoint, theme, layout | Solid store + localStorage |
| Historical data | Bar arrays | Source plugins → store.bars |
| Live data | Bar updates | Stream plugins → multiplex → bars/chart |
| Calculation | script + bars → RunResult | Engine plugins |
| Library | ScriptDocument | Storage plugins |
| Telemetry | plane connectivity / latency / ticks | Ephemeral store.telemetry + Connection HUD |
Transport preference (ADR-014)
| Path | Preferred transport | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Load (history) | REST / local | Venue kline HTTP, CSV, mock walk |
| Live | WebSocket | Venue WSS with reconnect; mock-poll local; DO = brokered |
| Engine server | HTTP | POST /run batch; latency in HUD |
| Engine pyodide | Local | Offline-capable after asset cache |
Live pairs with history via defaultStreamForSource. live.preferAfterLoad auto-starts WS after Load and on boot when bars exist (default on).
Built-in engines
| Engine | Transport | Fidelity host |
| --- | --- | --- |
| server | WebSocket /ws/run, then POST /run | Flask Pro API or AXIS Worker |
| pyne-worker | POST /run (optional WS) | HOOX pyne-worker. Built-in. Not a pasted Backend URL on server. |
| pyodide | In-browser Python | Self-hosted Pyodide 0.29.5 + vendored wheel + pynescript_runtime.py |
Timeout policy in runner scales with bar length (clamped). Live re-runs use shorter silent timeouts.
Chart apply pipeline
runAndApply:
1. getActiveEngine().run(...)
2. setLastRun
3. Optionally open results
4. Sync overlays in place (syncOverlayLines) — no blank destroy frame
5. Markers + strategy report side effects
6. Atomic Pine script drawings replace
7. Equity pane when appropriate (skip hide thrash on silent live re-runs)
History vs live data path: loadBars bumps chartDataGen → ChartHost full setDataToChart + fit. Live ticks only manager.appendBar (never full setData/fitContent).
PaneManager is imperative DOM under a Solid-owned outer shell—Solid must not reconcile the pane root (ChartHost invariant).
Plugin registry
PluginRegistry holds ordered maps per kind; events registered / unregistered. Built-ins register once (ensureBuiltins / catalog ensureRegistered). Dynamic plugins via loadPluginFromUrl. Active selection is not inside the registry—it is store state.
Contract namespace conceptually: pynescript.axis.plugins.v1 (fields: id, name, kind, configSchema, capabilities, kind-specific methods).
Edge plane (optional)
Cloudflare® split:
Pages project axis (https://axis.hoox.sh): static dist/
Worker script worker-axis (https://worker.axis.hoox.sh): /api/run proxy, keys/usage KV, scripts D1, stream DO fan-out
AXIS still speaks engine/storage plugins—not raw CF APIs in UI components
Invariants
1. AXIS ≠ engine
2. Bars/logs/lastRun not fully persisted
3. Storage and engine endpoints may coincide (Worker) but remain separate plugin roles
4. Older keys migrate forward, never the reverse on write
Failure modes (architectural)
| Mode | Manifestation | Mitigation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| God-object UI | Logic in components | Keep runner/registry pure modules |
| Engine leakage | Fetch /run from random widgets | Only engine plugins perform calc I/O |
| Registry/store split brain | Flat engine ≠ activePlugins.engine | setActivePlugin keeps alignment |
| SPA asset fallback | Pyodide loads HTML | Assert ZIP magic on assets |
See also
ADRs
Topologies
State namespaces
AXIS store

title: "Performance & architecture audit — Q4 2026"
description: "Prioritized gap analysis for turning AXIS into a production-grade, super-snappy, modern charting application."
---
Performance & architecture audit — Q4 2026
1. Baseline health
The repo is already in a strong performance culture:
Type check passes (bun run typecheck clean).
Unit + worker test suite passes: 4708 pass / 10 skip / 0 fail.
lint finishes with zero errors (warnings only).
A dedicated firehose bench exists under AXIS_BENCH=1 (tests/bench/).
The v1 live chart path has explicit frame-budget guardrails (e2e/v1-chart.spec.ts, 50 ms panFrame ceiling).
CHANGELOG.md shows recent O(n) fixes (pre-eval call scanning, bar append path no longer re-sanitizes clean bars, windowed merges are linear).
The objective now is to move from "already good" to "absolutely world-class": sub-16 ms feedback, 60 FPS during live streaming + pan/zoom, and a UI/UX that feels like a premium commercial product.
2. Architecture strengths to preserve
1. Plugin boundary is respected. AXIS ≠ engine is enforced through src/plugins/, src/engines/, src/workers/. No Solid component imports PYNE directly.
2. Live/paint split is explicit. src/chart/live-append.ts owns the only chart paint path from a live tick, with coalesced flushes and ranging-aware setData suppression.
3. Data Source Manager is backgrounded. src/data/data-source-manager.ts does all network paging on detached async jobs; PAGE_YIELD_MS keeps a page from starving the event loop.
4. Per-slot runtime is separated from global state. src/chart/chart-registry.ts keeps per-slot bars/gen in a dedicated store while managers/layers are imperative refs.
5. Lazy UI loading. src/app.tsx splits heavy shells (StudioHost, EditorPane, CommandPalette, panels) with lazy().
3. Top bottlenecks & weaknesses
3.1 Reactivity surface too coarse
Evidence: src/store/index.ts is 1,300+ lines and exports many mutation helpers. loadBars (src/store/index.ts:3252) uses batch() but still replaces store.bars with a fresh array, which notifies any component/effect that reads store.bars.
Impact: App.tsx updates document.title on every bar update (bars effect, src/app.tsx:179), and ChartHost.tsx has ~15 createEffects that read global store paths. A live tick causes a chain of effect runs even when nothing visible changes.
Severity: High.
3.2 ChartHost is an effect-dense component
Evidence: src/chart/ChartHost.tsx contains 11+ createEffects with untrack() fences. Several effects read store.chartDataGen/store.bars.length per live append to guard overlays/drawings/pins. Each effect re-evaluates independently.
Impact: More effect scheduling means more microtasks, especially during streaming pan/zoom.
Severity: High.
3.3 Overlay updates are unconditional
Evidence: Compare overlay effect (src/chart/ChartHost.tsx:529) calls applyCompareOverlay() whenever tracked compare state changes, even if the visible OHLC suffix is identical. On-chain overlay effect (src/chart/ChartHost.tsx:573) maps a full reactive array of points on every chartDataGen bump.
Impact: Redundant full-series writes to LWC, GC pressure, possible frame drops.
Severity: Medium-High.
3.4 Live tick still propagates to non-visual store paths
Evidence: appendBar (src/store/index.ts:3564) sets store.live.needsRerun and store.live.lastBarTime on every accepted tick. These paths are read by the live re-run scheduler and, transitively, by indicator state.
Impact: Thrashing indicator state / re-run bookkeeping on every tick while eval is still running, causing unnecessary work.
Severity: Medium.
3.5 Engine response parsing is main-thread synchronous
Evidence: src/engines/pro.ts, src/workers/pyodide-client.ts, and src/engine/ pass JSON back to the main thread and parse with JSON.parse. Large strategy/equity arrays block the main thread.
Impact: A large backtest or deep indicator run can freeze the chart for 50–300 ms while the result is deserialized and applied.
Severity: High.
3.6 Memory pressure from long-lived transient objects
Evidence: validateJobBars (src/data/data-source-manager.ts:195-229) still calls repairBars on the dataset when sorting is suspected. Array slices in applyCompareOverlay, applyOnchainOverlays and series-factory.ts allocate new objects on every effect run.
Impact: Extra GC on long sessions / 50k+ datasets.
Severity: Medium.
3.7 UI theming and layout effects are not batched
Evidence: src/ui/panels/DockColumn.tsx reads store.panelChrome in three separate memos (ids, width, bottomHeight) plus getHoverSlideExpandedMap(). src/chart/ChartHost.tsx:448 schedules a reflow effect on store.panelChrome and store.chartLayout.mode.
Impact: A single panel resize may trigger multiple effect passes and reflow RAFs.
Severity: Medium.
3.8 Accessibility & polish gaps
Evidence: Micro-interactions rely on ad-hoc inline Tailwind classes (e.g., src/ui/shortcuts/Feedback.tsx uses a hard-coded 1200 ms timeout and no prefers-reduced-motion). Many modal/panel components do not expose aria-labelledby routes. Focus traps are implemented per-component rather than through a single focus manager.
Impact: Power users notice inconsistent feedback; keyboard/screen-reader users hit friction.
Severity: Medium.
4. Prioritized improvement plan
Phase 0 — Measure first (week 1)
1. Add runtime telemetry hooks:
performance.mark around appendBar, setDataToChart, engine request/response, and JSON parse.
Track effect-count per tick using a dev-only ProbeStore wrapper.
Record panFrame / rAF budgets from the existing e2e harness.
2. Create a profiling issue template and baseline dashboard entry.
3. Establish decision rule: no Phase 1 change ships without a before/after measurement.
Phase 1 — Reactive architecture (weeks 2–3)
1. Decouple bars from the giant app store.
Move the canonical OHLCV series and live-tip metadata out of store.bars into a dedicated, narrow signal/store (src/stores/bars.ts).
Keep AppState as UI orchestration state only; derive any UI reads from the narrow store.
2. Audit every store.bars reader.
Replace createEffect(() => { const n = store.bars.length; ... }) with fine-grained derived signals (tip, lastPrice, dataGen).
Throttle document.title updates to 250 ms or only on tip closure.
3. Coalesce ChartHost effects.
Merge overlay and drawing effects where possible; use a single createEffect with a discriminated notification object.
Guard every chart write behind a deep-equality check for the visible data window.
4. Batch store mutations.
Wrap every public mutation helper in batch() where not already done.
Eliminate dual writes to legacy flat flags + panelChrome in the same turn.
Phase 2 — Rendering & data-plane performance (weeks 3–4)
1. Incremental series updates.
Make applyCompareOverlay, applyOnchainOverlays, and indicator apply paths compute a diff and only call series.update / setMarkers for changed data.
Use typed arrays or shared flat buffers for large numeric series when calling into LWC.
2. Ranging-aware overlay writes.
Suppress overlay/marker full-rewrites while isRanging() is true, mirroring the candle slide logic.
3. Lazy bar payloads.
Reuse arrays between store and slot cache by slicing from the same immutable ring buffer; avoid unwrap() copies on each append.
4. GC hygiene.
Pool transient arrays in heavy paths (indicator apply preview, on-chain overlays) with size caps.
Phase 3 — Engine boundary improvements (weeks 4–5)
1. Main-thread deser offloading.
For large responses, parse engine JSON in a Worker (src/workers/json-parse.worker.ts) or use streaming JSON.parse (async chunked parser) for result arrays > 100 KB.
2. Typed-result contracts.
Add engine result envelopes that separate metadata from numeric series, enabling transfer of typed arrays and reducing string overhead.
3. Pyodide streaming.
Evaluate whether TransformStream can push back partial line/mark results as they are produced, instead of one huge JSON string.
4. Result caching.
Cache indicator evaluation for unchanged (scriptId, barsGen, inputs); invalidate only when one of those changes.
Phase 4 — UX / visual polish (weeks 5–6)
1. Design system hardening.
Ensure every color/spacing/radius uses the void Tailwind theme tokens in src/index.css (@theme); delete leftover hard-coded color literals.
Add prefers-reduced-motion support globally; remove layout-property CSS transitions and rely on transform/opacity only.
2. Focus & keyboard system.
Introduce a central focus manager for modals/panels/drawers (src/ui/focus-trap.ts) and use it from Modal.tsx, StudioHost, AlertsPanel.
Add visible focus rings that match the design tokens; audit focus order in the chart/workspace.
3. Loading and feedback states.
Unify skeletons/spinners under one component hierarchy.
Improve shortcut feedback to respect reduced motion and debounce rapid chords.
4. Mobile / responsive refinements.
Verify bottom-sheet panel portals honor safe areas; reduce touch-target fragility on the toolbar.
Phase 5 — Continuous quality gates (week 6+)
1. Add a bun run perf:audit script that runs unit/e2e + records selected benchmark numbers.
2. Add a CI gate that fails if AXIS_BENCH=1 firehose exceeds a budget.
3. Document ADR for the new narrow bars store and result-streaming contracts.
4. Keep CHANGELOG.md updated per checkpoint rule.
5. Validation metrics
| Metric | Current target | World-class target |
| --- | --- | --- |
| appendBar → paint latency (p99) | 90 |
| Unit test pass rate | 100% | 100% with perf gates |
6. Risks & mitigations
| Risk | Mitigation |
| --- | --- |
| Splitting the store breaks persistence shape | Keep store.bars as a derived view for migration; persist canonical data through the narrow store only if needed |
| Off-thread JSON parsing increases latency for small results | Only offload when payload > threshold (50–100 KB) |
| Typed-array result contracts require backend changes | Gate behind feature flags; server-only first, Worker later |
| Too many UI polish changes regress accessibility | Add a11y lint / Playwright a11y spec |
7. Related files
Store: src/store/index.ts, src/chart/chart-registry.ts
Rendering: src/chart/ChartHost.tsx, src/chart/live-append.ts, src/chart/series-factory.ts, src/chart/manager-access.ts
Data plane: src/data/data-source-manager.ts, src/data/merge-datasets.ts, src/data/dataset-validate.ts
Engine boundary: src/engines/pro.ts, src/engine/router.ts, src/workers/pyodide-client.ts, src/workers/pyodide-protocol.ts
UI shell: src/app.tsx, src/ui/panels/DockColumn.tsx, src/ui/shortcuts/Feedback.tsx, src/index.css
Editor: src/editor/PyneEditor.tsx
Tests/bench: tests/bench/, e2e/v1-chart.spec.ts
8. Phase 0 / Phase 1 implementation notes
Updated during the first pass — these items have landed on the development branch.
Phase 0 — Instrumentation (landed)
Added a tiny telemetry module at src/perf/telemetry.ts that wraps performance.mark / measure and only activates in dev/test builds.
Placed marks/measures on the current dominant costs so future work has a before/after signal:
src/store/index.ts: perfMark('axis:loadBars:setStore'), perfMark('axis:appendBar:store').
src/chart/manager-access.ts: perfMeasureSync('axis:setDataToChart', …) around the full paint path.
src/engines/json-sanitize.ts: perfMeasureSync('axis:parseEngineJson', …) around the shared engine response parser.
Deviations from the plan: the dev-only ProbeStore wrapper and effect-count probe were deferred. The existing chart.lag/chart.bars MCP app capabilities already expose live latency data, so we are using those plus the new marks for initial profiling.
Phase 1 — Narrow bar plane (landed)
Created src/stores/bars.ts with four fine-grained accessors:
bars — full visible window (readers can still subscribe to the whole array).
tip — the forming/newest bar; updates independently of bars on every live tick.
length — window size; only changes on grow/slide, not on every tick.
gen — full-reload generation; bumped by loadBars/slot restores and rolling-window slides.
Wired the plane from the existing authoritative paths:
loadBars pushes the validated view into barPlane (with symbol/interval/exchange).
appendBar pushes appends and slides into barPlane (bumpGen: true only for slides).
restoreSlotBarsToActivePlane restores slot cache via barPlane.
flushLiveFrame updates only barPlane.tip when a forming tick lands.
Discovered and fixed a Solid-store proxy-link issue during the migration: when the same raw bar object was placed both in barPlane.bars and in barPlane.tip, and that object was also an element of store.bars, the two stores' proxies could interfere with the app store's closed mutation during index appends. The fix is to clone the plane's tip in setBarPlaneFull so the narrow plane never exposes the exact same object instance as both the tail of bars and the live tip.
Migrated the highest-frequency UI readers from store.bars / store.bars.length to barPlane:
Shell chrome: App.tsx (document title, rAF-throttled), StatusBar.tsx, Topbar.tsx.
Float/overlay panels: PricePanel.tsx, DataViewPanel.tsx, QuotePanel.tsx, VolumeProfileOverlay.tsx, TimePanel.tsx.
Extras/modals: CurrentPriceCard.tsx, HpoPanel.tsx, BarReplayControls.tsx.
store.bars remains the canonical source for engine evaluation, legacy components, and ChartHost's full paint path during this migration. Phase 2 will decide whether to move the paint path to the plane or to the slot cache.
Tooling updates
package.json now exposes bun check as the canonical typecheck command (root, worker, and CLI). The old bunx tsc --noEmit is retired for the standard alias.
Fixed packages/cli/tsconfig.json where baseUrl now emits a TS5102 error; replaced it with "paths": { "": ["./"] } so typecheck:all passes cleanly.
Validation after Phase 0/1
bun run typecheck:all — clean (563 app + 29 worker + 39 CLI files).
bun run lint — exits 0 (warnings remain; no errors).
bun run test:unit — 4708 pass / 10 skip / 0 fail.
Introduced zero new test failures; the only transient regression was the existing store-closed-on-new-bar.test.ts asserting closed mutation persistence, which exposed the proxy-link bug above and was fixed before commit.
Phase 2 — Rendering & data-plane performance (landed)
Bar-plane append no longer copies the whole window. Added a targeted setBarPlaneAppend(bar, index) path in src/stores/bars.ts used by appendBar for the common in-place append. The previous full-array setBarPlaneFull is still used for initial loads, slot restores, and rolling-window slides, plus a fallback when the plane length has drifted from store.bars (e.g. tests that write store.bars directly).
setDataToChart skips duplicate volume writes. When the candle series paint is skipped because the bar window is unchanged (skipSeries), the volume series setData is now skipped too — it derives from the same bars.
Compare overlay avoids identical setData. applyCompareOverlay (src/chart/compare-overlay.ts) now caches the last result from buildCompareSeriesData and short-circuits the LWC write when the built data is identical (which happens on unrelated chartDataGen/label-pref effect re-runs).
On-chain scalar overlays avoid identical setData. applyOnchainOverlays (src/chart/onchain-overlay.ts) fingerprints each line's point array and skips series.setData when the fingerprint matches the last paint, while still re-applying visibility options.
On-chain event markers avoid full rebuilds. applyOnchainEventMarkers (src/chart/onchain-events.ts) fingerprints the event set and skips the invisible host-series setData/marker-plugin rebuild when unchanged.
Ranging-aware overlay suppression in ChartHost. Added a reactive isRanging signal in src/chart/ChartHost.tsx that polls PaneManager.isRanging() while mounted. The compare, on-chain scalar, and on-chain event effects now suppress full writes while the user is panning/zooming and flush the latest state once the gesture ends.
Validation after Phase 2
bun run typecheck:all — clean.
bun run lint — exits 0.
bun run test:unit — 4708 pass / 10 skip / 0 fail.
Phase 3 — Engine-boundary performance (landed)
Off-thread engine JSON parsing. Added parseEngineJsonAsync in src/engines/json-sanitize.ts and a dedicated src/engines/json-parse-worker.ts. Payloads above 256 KB are parsed in a module Worker using the same sanitizeNonFiniteJson logic, so main-thread blocking drops for large /run, /run/batch, and Pyodide result strings. Wired into:
src/engines/live-sessions.ts (create/tick/append/status)
src/engines/engine-ws.ts (inbound WebSocket frames)
src/engines/catalog.ts (REST /run and Pyodide local path)
src/engines/batch.ts (batch /run/batch envelope)
Typed-result contract for Pyodide. The local Pyodide path now runs its result JSON through parseEngineJson and lifts it with normalizeEngineResult (src/indicators/run-helpers.ts) before returning, so REST/WS/Pyodide all present the same NormalizedRunResult shape to the apply pipeline.
In-memory result cache keyed by (scriptId, barsGen, inputs). New src/indicators/run-cache.ts memoizes successful non-live runs using a stable fingerprint of engine id, script id/script source hash, resolved inputs, engine config, bar-plane generation, bar window length, and resolved library sources. It is bounded (16 entries, 30 s TTL), clones cached values on store and retrieve to protect apply-stage mutations, and is automatically disabled in test environments to avoid cross-test pollution. Wired into runScript in src/indicators/runner.ts.
Pyodide streaming. True chunked/incremental result streaming is still outstanding; the immediate win is that the final large Pyodide JSON string now parses off the main thread. Streaming evaluation remains on the Phase 5 backlog.
Validation after Phase 3
bun run typecheck:all — clean.
bun run lint — exits 0.
bun run test:unit — 4708 pass / 10 skip / 0 fail.
Phase 4 — UX / visual polish (landed)
Motion + safe-area tokens. @theme now owns --ui-motion-duration-fast/base/slow and --ui-safe-top/bottom/left/right. Raw env(safe-area-inset-) call sites (mobile chrome, dialog sheets, FloatableShell phone geometry) read the tokens. Reduced motion collapses the duration tokens to 0.01ms on :root, html and globally shortens animation/transition duration so transitionend still fires.
Drawing rail is compositor-only. .axis-draw-rail fades with opacity var(--ui-motion-duration-fast) instead of max-height / max-width. Hover-slide layout transitions (width / height / flex-basis / min-width) go through hoverSlideTransition() and are skipped when prefersReducedMotion() is true; CSS also sets transition: none on .axis-panel-hover-slide under reduced motion. Peek/expand still happens, instantly.
Theme tokens on chart scale chrome. ChartScaleControls uses --color-border, --color-accent, --color-text(-dim), --color-bg-panel instead of hard-coded hex.
Shared Spinner. src/ui/Spinner.tsx wraps the loader icon with role="status", aria-busy, a stable data-testid="axis-spinner", and a default "Loading" label. Wired into IndicatorCard, OnChainPanel, ResultsModal, ScriptSettingsModal, StorageChangeDialog, and SymbolModal. Busy refresh* glyphs keep Icons.refresh + animate-spin.
Coarse-pointer topbar targets. @media (pointer: coarse) raises command-bar / module / engine-switch buttons to a 40×40 px minimum with touch-action: manipulation.
Modal focus restore + aria-hidden siblings. installFocusTrap accepts returnElement and hides ancestor-chain siblings with aria-hidden="true" while the trap is active (already-hidden nodes stay hidden; previous values including false are restored on dispose). Solid ref fires before insert, so the sibling walk retries on a microtask once the dialog is in-tree. Modal passes returnElement through. Dispose order: unhide, then focus returnElement or the previously focused element. Missing document.body is a no-op so test doubles keep working.
Validation after Phase 4
bun run typecheck:all — clean (566 app + 29 worker + 39 CLI files).
bun run lint — exits 0 (warnings remain; no errors). New !important on the global reduced-motion rule is intentional.
Phase 4 pins: tests/spinner.test.ts, tests/phase4-ux-tokens.test.ts, plus extensions of tests/focus-trap.test.ts, tests/ui-modal-shell.test.ts, tests/panel-hover-slide.test.ts — 36 pass.
bun run test:unit — 4723 pass / 10 skip / 1 fail. The failure is data-source-manager > applyCachedToChart with onlyIfCurrent skips paint for a stale symbol timing out at 5 s (reproduced in isolation at ~6 s). It does not import Phase 4 UI files; treat it as a pre-existing slow DSM path, not a Phase 4 regression.
Phase 5 — Continuous quality gates (landed)
bun run perf:audit. scripts/perf-audit.mjs runs typecheck, a hot-path unit slice (store-append-scale, live-append, store-live-frame, paint-latency), then AXIS_BENCH=1 bun test tests/bench/. It scrapes AXIS_BENCH {json} lines from bench stdout and writes reports/perf-audit.json (gitignored). --full / bun run perf:audit:full also runs test:unit and Playwright @smoke. --skip-typecheck / --skip-bench exist for iterating on one side.
CI firehose budget. .github/workflows/ci.yml adds a parallel Firehose budget job: bun run test:bench with AXIS_BENCH=1, 10-minute job timeout. The existing expect(sample.ms).toBeLessThan(FIREHOSE_SOFT_BUDGET_MS) (120 s) fails the job if the 500k-tick append path regresses into minutes. Dataset linear-merge keeps its 5 s soft budget.
ADR-017 — Narrow bar plane. docs/architecture/adrs.mdx records barPlane (bars / tip / length / gen), the clone-on-full-write rule, and that store.bars stays the engine/eval source during migration.
ADR-018 — Engine result parse + typed contract. Records the 256 KB Worker parse threshold, normalizeEngineResult as the apply envelope, and the bounded run-cache. Chunked Pyodide evaluation stays future work.
Validation after Phase 5
bun run typecheck:all — clean (566 app + 29 worker + 39 CLI files).
Phase 5 pins: tests/perf-audit.test.ts — 9 pass.
bun run perf:audit --skip-typecheck (this host, 2026-11-01): hot-path unit 441 ms; appendBar firehose 500k ticks in 19.1 s (26.1k ops/s, budget 120 s); dataset linear merge 50 pages in 829 ms (budget 5 s). Report: reports/perf-audit.json.
9. Next move
The six-phase audit plan is implemented through quality gates. Follow-ups that remain open: chunked Pyodide result streaming, typed-array engine envelopes, a nightly axis-nightly.yml if full e2e should stay off PR CI, and shrinking the DSM applyCachedToChart timeout. Keep requiring before/after measurements for any new hot-path change.

title: "State namespaces"
description: "AXIS persistence keys, key migration, pluginsConfig, editor docs, library IDB, and URL hash state."
---
State namespaces
AXIS persistence is multi-homed: layout/config in localStorage, script library in IndexedDB (or remotes), ephemeral run data in memory, optional URL hash for shareable slices.
Abstract
| Namespace | Medium | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| pynescript.axis.v1 | localStorage | App shell state |
| pynescript.axis.editor.doc | localStorage | Editor document backup |
| pynescript.axis.storage | IndexedDB | Local library |
| pynescript.axis.library.v1 | localStorage fallback | Library if no IDB |
| pynescript.axis.library.draft | localStorage | Draft buffer |
| Plugin install list | localStorage | URL plugins restore |
| store.bars / lastRun / logs | memory | Session only |
Contract / product identifiers (not storage keys):
``text
pynescript.axis.plugins.v1 # conceptual plugin contract id
axis # CF Pages project id
axis.hoox.sh # Pages custom domain
worker-axis # CF Worker script id
worker.axis.hoox.sh # Worker custom domain
`
Conceptual model
App state key pynescript.axis.v1
Solid store (src/store/index.ts):
Persisted (representative):
Market: symbol, interval, exchange, source, engine, endpoint
activePlugins { source, stream, engine, storage }
pluginsConfig
Layout: theme, editor, watchlist (named lists + active symbols), panel open/width/height
panes, scripts (indicator list metadata)
live.streamId (and related live flags carefully)
drawings (user annotations)
Flat mirrors: source / engine aligned via setActivePlugin
Explicitly omitted on persist:
bars
lastRun
logs
Debounced write (~200ms).
Legacy migration
Read order:
1. pynescript.axis.v1
2. else pynescript.axis.v2
On older-key hit: copy forward into pynescript.axis.v1.
The pre-Solid vanilla state.js shell (removed in 2.21.0) used the same STORAGE_KEY constant. It is gone; only the Solid store path remains.
pluginsConfig
Map of configuration objects. Preferred keys:
`text
${kind}:${id} e.g. storage:cloud, storage:git, engine:server
`
Bare ids may still be read for compatibility (cloud, git). Values feed configSchema resolution inside plugins.
Sensitive fields: apiKey, git token — browser-local; treat profile as secret store.
Editor document
| Key | Role |
| --- | --- |
| pynescript.axis.editor.doc | Draft / shared doc |
| Bridge messages | Popout synchronization |
Popout mode uses editor-bridge + shared storage so detached windows share source without re-fetching library.
Local library IDB
| Constant | Value |
| --- | --- |
| DB name | pynescript.axis.storage |
| Version | 1 |
| Stores | scripts, kv |
Migration flags (pynescript.axis.library.migrated) prevent re-import loops from older library keys:
pynescript.axis.library.v1
pynescript.axis.library.legacy
Cloud / git (remote namespaces)
Not browser keys—server-side partitions:
| Backend | Partitioning |
| --- | --- |
| cloud | Hash of API key on Worker; D1 table or memory |
| git | owner/repo@branch + basePath |
Revisions: cloud may expose revision / If-Match; git uses blob SHAs via forge APIs.
URL hash state (legacy helper)
src/state-hash.js (legacy state path):
| Feature | Detail |
| --- | --- |
| Keys | symbol, interval, engine, source, stream, timeRange |
| Script | base64 in hash if short |
| Cap | ~2000 chars |
| API | applyHashState, pushHashState, watchHashState |
Solid-first product may not wire this by default; treat as optional share mechanism. Prefer library export + recipe docs for durable shares.
Invariants
1. Migration is read-repair to AXIS keys.
2. Never persist full OHLCV in axis.v1.
3. setActivePlugin keeps flat fields coherent.
4. Logs panel open state forced closed on hydrate (noise control).
5. Drawing tool resets to cursor on hydrate; geometries restore.
Failure modes
| Issue | Effect | Mitigation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| QuotaExceeded | persist no-op | Export library; shrink drawings |
| Poisoned JSON | load defaults | Security tests; clear key |
| Split profiles | “missing” library | Same browser profile |
| Stale activePlugins id | missing plugin | Fall back / reselect built-in |
Internals
| Path | Role |
| --- | --- |
| src/store/index.ts | Solid persistence |
| src/store/types.ts | AppState |
| src/state.js | Legacy state |
| src/state-hash.js | Hash sync |
| src/storage/local.ts | IDB library |
| src/plugins/loader.ts` | Installed plugin list |
See also
ADR-011
AXIS store
Script library

---
title: "Topologies"
description: "AXIS deploy and dev topologies: Vite+Flask, static PWA, Cloudflare® Pages+Worker, offline Pyodide lab, and local Bun sidecar."
---
Topologies
Concrete process and network shapes for AXIS. Choose a topology; then compose plugins inside it (recipes).
Abstract
| Topology | AXIS | Calc | Live | Library |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Dev desk | Vite :3000 | Flask :5002 | Binance WS direct | local |
| Static demo | axis_pwa_server :8081 | Flask or remote | venue / mock | local |
| Edge | CF Pages | Worker → Flask | DO fan-out optional | cloud |
| Offline lab | any AXIS | Pyodide | mock-poll | local |
| Dev desk + sidecar | Vite :3000 | Flask :5002 | Bun sidecar :5003 | local |
Topology A — Dev desk
Bring-up
``bash
make run
bun install && bun run dev
`
Notes: Vite may proxy /run; Settings endpoint can still point absolute at :5002. CORS must allow Vite origin.
Topology B — Static PWA + Pro API
`bash
bun run build
python3 axis_pwa_server.py
make run # separate process
`
VPS demo pattern: systemd units for axis-pwa and pynescript-api; ALLOWED_ORIGINS includes AXIS origin.
Topology C — Cloudflare® AXIS at the edge
Deploy sketch
`bash
axis deploy
or: cd worker && bun run deploy
bun run build
wrangler pages deploy dist --project-name=axis
`
Ids: Pages project axis at https://axis.hoox.sh; Worker script worker-axis at https://worker.axis.hoox.sh (ADR-008).
AXIS config:
Engine server, endpoint = Worker origin
Storage cloud, same origin + API key
Stream: direct venue or DO-backed plugin
Topology D — Offline lab
No Flask, no venue. Requires vendored pyodide + wheels on origin once.
Topology E — Git-centric research
Any of A–C for calc/data; storage axis = git. Forges are orthogonal network peers:
`text
AXIS --storage:git--> api.github.com | gitlab
AXIS --engine:server--> Flask/Worker
AXIS --source--> venue or CSV
`
Topology F — Dev desk + Bun sidecar (CCXT Pro)
For users who want authenticated WS feeds from any CCXT-supported exchange without running Flask. The sidecar (packages/datafeed/) mirrors the PYNE datafeed gateway contract.
`bash
bun run dev # Vite :3000
bun run dev:datafeed # Bun sidecar :5003
in separate terminals, or combine in a Procfile
`
Gateway contract (same as PYNE datafeed):
| Endpoint | Method | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| /datafeed/ohlcv?exchange=&symbol=&timeframe= | GET | Historical OHLCV |
| /datafeed/markets?exchange= | GET | Available markets |
| /datafeed/watch?exchange=&symbol=&timeframe= | WS | Live bar stream |
| /datafeed/session | POST | Store credentials (RAM only) |
| /health | GET | Sidecar health |
Notes: Credentials stay in RAM only (never persisted). The sidecar is optional — public venues work without it via direct browser WS.
Port map (defaults)
| Service | Port |
| --- | --- |
| Vite dev | 3000 |
| Flask Pro API | 5002 |
| Bun datafeed sidecar | 5003 |
| Static PWA server | 8081 |
| Wrangler Worker | 8787 |
Comparison
| Concern | A Dev | B Static | C Edge | D Offline | F Sidecar |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| HMR | yes | no | no | n/a | yes |
| PWA SW realism | partial | full | full | full | partial |
| Multi-tenant keys | no | no | yes | no | local only |
| Ops complexity | low | medium | high | low | low |
| Fidelity | high (Flask) | high | high via proxy | engine-dependent | high (CCXT Pro) |
Failure modes by topology
| Topology | Typical break |
| --- | --- |
| A | Flask not running; CORS localhost vs 127.0.0.1 mismatch |
| B | Stale SW; missing dist pyodide assets |
| C | Unbound KV/D1; wrong project name; EXTERNAL_BACKEND down |
| D | First visit offline; SPA fallback for wheels |
Internals
| Path | Role |
| --- | --- |
| vite.config.ts | Dev/build |
| axis_pwa_server.py | Static host |
| worker/wrangler.toml | CF name + bindings |
| packages/cli/ | AXIS CLI (setup / deploy / health) |
| worker/README.md` | Edge ops |
See also
ADR-008, 009, 010, 012
